//function ajaxSearchKlevSubmit(form) {

//    if (value === '') {

function ajaxsearchsubmit(form) {
    var value = form.w.value.replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g, '');
    if (value === '') {
        return false;
    }
    var search = encodeURIComponent(value);
    var url = form.getElementById("hiddenUrl").value;
    document.activeElement.blur();
    window.location = url + "?w=" + search;
    return false;
}

</script>

		}
	});
